All but `Asolaria` were read from bare clones made with `--filter=blob:none`: trees and commits are local, and each blob was fetched when it was read. Every file was read with `git cat-file -p <blob>` and checked three ways before it was kept: `git rev-parse <commit>:<path>` equals the recorded blob, `git hash-object --no-filters` of the written file equals it (410 of 410), and its sha256 equals the inventory's. `sha256` below is the first 16 hex of the file's sha256 (full digests in `UPSTREAM.sha256`, `sha256sum -c` form, run from this directory; 410 of 410 OK). `source @ commit : path` names the commit the bytes were read from; for every row it is the earliest commit on the repository's branches and tags (pull-request refs aside) that put that blob at that path.

`beginning` names the commit that first introduced that exact blob, at any path, on any ref (committer time, -03:00). It was measured from `git log --all --raw --no-renames` over every local ref plus every GitHub ref the remote lists (`git ls-remote`), including the `refs/pull/*/head` refs; all of those commits are present in the clones. Where a pull-request head carried the bytes before the merge, `beginning` is the earlier pull-request commit (see the section after the table). Every commit a remote ref points to is in the clones except three `Asolaria` commits made after the mirror: `179d2ca` (`main`), `3214094` (`ccr-7180d4f5-bq2h43` and `refs/pull/26/head`) and the merge ref `ffa6e71`; they and their ancestry back to the mirror (`af6c88a`, `aad4bfd`, `2526b30`, `e51479b`, `981a757`) are dated 2026-10-18, so they change no date here.

Paths: `vendor/<repo>/<path at the commit>`. Where a later version of the same path is also kept, it is under `vendor/<repo>-variant-<commit7>/<path>` (25 files in 18 variant directories). Same-day revisions are pinned by blob in the last part of this file, not vendored, with two exceptions that keep their same-day final state: the rime fixed-27 crate at `02bd37b` and the four USIM documents the gap map names by their final blobs (`RESULTS.md` c23be594, `ASOLARIA-EXTRACTED-VALUES.md` 5b4d5803, `LAW-38-GLYPH-TONGUES-COMMAND-TUPLES.md` 74879589, `PID-TUPLE-GLYPH-LANGUAGE-2026-07-28.md` 1f54b310).
